# TABLE: entity_google_reviews   -   5 row(s)

--- entity_google_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
review_id: 3205
entity_slug: agave-bar-grill
google_review_name: places/ChIJyx5eQ3oFmogRTtnRSEt57Os/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT25GRVNWUkRhamRuVWxocGJWcDNXVE5IVTNSMU9VRRAB
rating: 2
review_text: First of all, the service was good so that was not the problem.

The food however, was an issue. I got the carne asada and asked for medium. What I received was a very well done and tough to eat carne asada. As you can see by the pictures, a big portion of it was just fat and gristle (on the plate). The rest was very tough and chewy. I'm not expecting a steakhouse cut, but it definitely seems that they used a very low quality piece of beef here. Yes, I finished it because I did not want to waste it but it was not good. Please do better.

reviewer_name: Aaron
rating: 2
body: First of all, the service was good so that was not the problem.

The food however, was an issue. I got the carne asada and asked for medium. What I received was a very well done and tough to eat carne asada. As you can see by the pictures, a big portion of it was just fat and gristle (on the plate). The rest was very tough and chewy. I'm not expecting a steakhouse cut, but it definitely seems that they used a very low quality piece of beef here. Yes, I finished it because I did not want to waste it but it was not good. Please do better.
